HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001281/B001","root_001281/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0a5a68b90458b1231463","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:24:4\",\"root_001281\",[\"B001\",\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Büyüklüğün ağır suçu da çağrıştırması, en büyük cezayı yüz çevirme ve inkârın ağırlığına göre ölçeklenen yargısal bir sonuç olarak gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000994/B005","root_001281/B001","root_001281/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1180187e42133b9cb44c","text":"2. **The greater punishment is scaled to a grave offense under account.** **Lexical:** `ك ب ر` B001, greatness by degree, and B007, \"major sin and grave offense\"; `ع ذ ب` B005 supplies consequence. **Cue:** turning away and rejection in 88:23 precede `ٱلْأَكْبَرَ`, while reckoning follows in 88:26. **Role:** the grave-offense branch supplies the seriousness to which the superlative consequence corresponds; account supplies the later evaluative mechanism. **Abduction:** I infer proportional scaling from offense, superlative, and account; no explicit tariff is given. **Reading change:** `الأكبر` becomes adjudicative magnitude, a punishment read in relation to the weight of rejection.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001281/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_303360ce746d327748f1","text":"3. **Mountain-like mass activates punishment as burden.** **Lexical:** `ك ب ر` B010, \"greatness as hardship or heaviness,\" alongside B001; `ج ب ل` B001 in 88:19 supplies a nearby high solid mass. **Cue:** the focus window begins with erected mountains and ends with the superlative punishment. **Role:** mountain mass gives perceptible weight to otherwise abstract greatness; B010 translates magnitude into what is hard to bear. **Abduction:** I infer a material analogy from the window's opening object. **Reading change:** the greater punishment is not only higher on a scale but crushingly weighty to its bearer.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001281/B005","root_001281/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5c47736bb3357f741cef","text":"4. **The superlative also marks superior jurisdiction.** **Lexical:** `ك ب ر` B005, rank, honor, and leadership, and B006, rightful grandeur, activated with divine `ء ل ه` B002. **Cue:** messenger-control is denied in 88:22, but God is the named agent in 88:24; return and account are then claimed in 88:25-26. **Role:** rank/grandeur locates the superior authority under which the punishment is called greater. **Abduction:** I infer jurisdictional hierarchy from agent contrast; grammatically `الأكبر` modifies punishment, not the agent. **Reading change:** the superlative carries an institutional as well as quantitative force: this is consequence from the authority beyond human control.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B002","root_000994/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_62a820eee50860888eee","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:24:1\",\"root_000994\",[\"B005\"]],[\"88:24:2\",\"root_000047\",[\"B002\"]],[\"88:24:3\",\"root_000994\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Cezalandırma fiili ile ceza adının art arda tekrarı ve Allah'ın açık özne oluşu, acıyı başıboş bir devam değil, insan denetimi reddedildikten sonra yetkili biçimde uygulanan sonuç yapar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B002","root_000994/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_66afa3c38a87c16598eb","text":"1. **Verb-noun repetition turns punishment into an expressly administered process.** **Lexical:** `ع ذ ب` B005, \"torment and punishment,\" occurring in both `يُعَذِّبُهُ` and `ٱلْعَذَابَ`; `ء ل ه` B002 identifies the fixed divine name. **Cue:** the object pronoun is enclosed by a cognate verb and noun, with `ٱللَّهُ` explicitly inserted as subject after human control was denied in 88:22. **Role:** the verb supplies action, the noun supplies its named class and emphasis, and the stated subject fixes jurisdiction. **Abduction:** I infer that cognate repetition marks deliberate administration rather than uncontrolled pain. **Reading change:** the ayah reads as a formally assigned consequence, not an anonymous continuation of suffering.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000006/B004","root_001281/B002","root_001507/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_69d98166b1e4615bf37c","text":"2. **Greater punishment as the chief burden.** Evidence: `ك ب ر` B002 chief share/bulk, B010 hardship/heaviness; `ن ص ب` B004 strain earlier; `ء ب ل` B004 heaviness/burden in camel inventory. Structural cue: fatigue, burden, and failed provision accumulate before this climax. Role: `الأكبر` names the maximal burden that gathers earlier pains. Abductive move: “greater” means not just larger degree but the main weight of consequence. Reading change: the punishment reads as the chief burden of the whole negative arc.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B002","root_000994/B005","root_001281/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6ea0e81ac463f1268d32","text":"1. **Punishment assigned to God, not the reminder.** Evidence: `ع ذ ب` B005 torment/punishment; `ء ل ه` B002 divine name; `ك ب ر` B001 greatness of degree. Structural cue: this follows `لست عليهم بمصيطر` and the exception of refusal. Role: Allah is the punishing agent; the messenger’s control remains negated. Abductive move: final coercive consequence must be divine if human domination is denied. Reading change: the ayah reads as agency clarification as much as threat.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7beb06e71d5823d69876","text":"4. **Womb/afterbirth echo under divine return.** Evidence: `ع ذب` B009 womb/afterbirth; opening `ر ح م` B003 womb; final `ء و ب` B001 return. Structural cue: divine punishment comes just before return/account. Role: this branch faintly links pain, emergence, and return to origin imagery. Abductive move: final accounting can reactivate birth/afterbirth vocabulary as a hard passage rather than comfort. Reading change: exploratory: punishment may carry a painful expulsion/afterbirth undertone beside juridical torment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_94cf99e606166c9bc52f","text":"The root’s sweet-water branch was surprising after the earlier `عين آنية`: punishment is lexically near sweetness that has been withheld or reversed. The final return/account also kept the divine agency of this ayah from being read as arbitrary heat.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000994/B001","root_000994/B002","root_000994/B003","root_000994/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_96a4f0e83f0d57dbe479","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:24:1\",\"root_000994\",[\"B001\",\"B002\",\"B003\",\"B005\"]],[\"88:24:3\",\"root_000994\",[\"B001\",\"B002\",\"B003\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Tatlı su, yeme içmeden kaçınma ve esirgeme çağrışımları, cezayı daha önceki kaynar içecek ile beslemeyen yiyecekte yararlı tadın tersine çevrilmesi ve geri çekilmesine bağlar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000994/B009"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a2afdc06bf88431b2be3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:24:1\",\"root_000994\",[\"B009\"]],[\"88:24:3\",\"root_000994\",[\"B009\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Rahim ve son çağrışımı, hemen sonraki dönüşle birleşince cezaya başlangıç noktasına dönüşten önceki sancılı bir çıkarılma gölgesi ekler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000994/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_beaf3adf8d24b0993091","text":"3. **Sweetness withheld and converted to torment.** Evidence: `ع ذ ب` B001 fresh sweet palatability, B002 abstention from food/drink, B003 withholding, B005 torment; earlier hot drink and non-food. Structural cue: `عذاب` follows a section where water and food fail. Role: the root carries both sweetness and torment, making deprivation part of punishment’s texture. Abductive move: torment may be experienced as the inversion or withholding of sweet drink/provision.
